Implement the following plan:

# Fix `entire explain` branch filtering with merge commits

## Context

`entire explain` branch filtering breaks when a feature branch has merge commits from main. The root cause: `repo.Log()` with `git.LogOrderCommitterTime` traverses ALL parents of merge commits (full DAG walk). After merging main into a feature branch, the walker enters main's full history. The `consecutiveMainLimit` (100) fires before older feature branch checkpoints are found, silently dropping them.

go-git v5's `LogOptions` has no `FirstParent` option, so we need manual first-parent traversal.

## Changes

### 1. Extract first-parent walk helper in `explain.go`

Create a `walkFirstParentCommits` helper that walks the first-parent chain from a starting hash, calling a visitor function for each commit. Stops at `commitScanLimit`.

```go
func walkFirstParentCommits(repo *git.Repository, from plumbing.Hash, limit int, fn func(*object.Commit) error) error
```

This replaces both `repo.Log()` call sites that walk the user's branch.

### 2. Simplify `getBranchCheckpoints()` (~explain.go:803)

**Remove:**
- `isOnDefault` / `mainBranchHash` / default branch resolution block (lines 830-849) — only needed for `reachableFromMain` filtering, which is no longer necessary
- `reachableFromMain` precomputation (lines 852-868)
- `consecutiveMainLimit` constant and `consecutiveMainCount` tracking (lines 883, 893-902)

**Replace:** `repo.Log()` at line 872 with `walkFirstParentCommits`. The visitor function only needs to extract checkpoint trailers and look up committed checkpoints — no main-branch skip logic needed.

**Keep:** `isOnDefault` is still needed for `getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints()` call at line 952. Compute it separately just for that.

### 3. Simplify `getAssociatedCommits()` (~explain.go:455)

**Remove:**
- `isOnDefault` / `mainBranchHash` block (lines 468-471)
- `reachableFromMain` precomputation (lines 474-490)
- The `reachableFromMain` skip check (line 512)

**Replace:** `repo.Log()` at line 492 with `walkFirstParentCommits`.

### 4. Update `isShadowBranchReachable()` (~explain.go:998)

**Replace:** `repo.Log()` at line 1005 with first-parent traversal for consistency. Shadow branch base commits should be on the first-parent chain if the session was started on this branch.

### 5. Clean up dead code

- Remove `consecutiveMainLimit` constant (line 791) if no longer referenced
- Remove `strategy.GetMainBranchHash()` import if unused after changes
- Remove `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName()` usage from `getBranchCheckpoints` if no longer needed there

### 6. Add regression test

Add `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_WithMergeFromMain` to `explain_test.go`:
- Create repo with main branch and several commits
- Create feature branch with a checkpoint commit
- Merge main into feature (creating a merge commit at HEAD)
- Add another feature checkpoint commit after the merge
- Call `getBranchCheckpoints()` — verify BOTH feature checkpoint commits appear
- This is the exact scenario that was broken

Also add `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_MergeCommitAtHEAD` to verify the case where HEAD itself is the merge commit.

## Files to modify

- `cmd/entire/cli/explain.go` — main changes (3 call sites + new helper + remove constants)
- `cmd/entire/cli/explain_test.go` — add regression tests

## Files NOT modified

- `checkpoint/temporary.go` `ListCheckpointsForBranch()` — walks shadow branches which are linear (no merges)
- `checkpoint/committed.go` `GetCheckpointAuthor()` — walks `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch; merge handling there is a separate concern

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

Specifically verify:
- New test `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_WithMergeFromMain` passes
- New test `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_MergeCommitAtHEAD` passes
- Existing tests `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_FiltersMainCommits` and `TestGetBranchCheckpoints_OnFeatureBranch` still pass
- Integration tests pass: `mise run test:integration`

   157→// runExplain routes to the appropriate explain function based on flags.
   158→func runExplain(w, errW io.Writer, sessionID, commitRef, checkpointID string, noPager, verbose, full, rawTranscript, generate, force, searchAll bool) error {
   159→	// Count mutually exclusive flags (--commit and --checkpoint are mutually exclusive)
   160→	// --session is now a filter for the list view, not a separate mode
   161→	flagCount := 0
   162→	if commitRef != "" {
   163→		flagCount++
   164→	}
   165→	if checkpointID != "" {
   166→		flagCount++
   167→	}
   168→	// If --session is combined with --commit or --checkpoint, that's still an error
   169→	if sessionID != "" && flagCount > 0 {
   170→		return errors.New("cannot specify multiple of --session, --commit, --checkpoint")
   171→	}
   172→	if flagCount > 1 {
   173→		return errors.New("cannot specify multiple of --session, --commit, --checkpoint")
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Route to appropriate handler
   177→	if commitRef != "" {
   178→		return runExplainCommit(w, commitRef, noPager, verbose, full, searchAll)
   179→	}
   180→	if checkpointID != "" {
   181→		return runExplainCheckpoint(w, errW, checkpointID, noPager, verbose, full, rawTranscript, generate, force, searchAll)
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Default or with session filter: show list view (optionally filtered by session)
   185→	return runExplainBranchWithFilter(w, noPager, sessionID)
   186→}
   187→
   188→// runExplainCheckpoint explains a specific checkpoint.
   189→// Supports both committed checkpoints (by checkpoint ID) and temporary checkpoints (by git SHA).
   190→// First tries to match committed checkpoints, then falls back to temporary checkpoints.
   191→// When generate is true, generates an AI summary for the checkpoint.
   192→// When force is true, regenerates even if a summary already exists.
   193→// When rawTranscript is true, outputs only the raw transcript file (JSONL format).
   194→// When searchAll is true, searches all commits without branch/depth limits (used for finding associated commits).
   195→func runExplainCheckpoint(w, errW io.Writer, checkpointIDPrefix string, noPager, verbose, full, rawTranscript, generate, force, searchAll bool) error {
   196→	repo, err := openRepository()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   202→
   203→	// First, try to find in committed checkpoints by checkpoint ID prefix
   204→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoints: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Collect all matching checkpoint IDs to detect ambiguity
   210→	var matches []id.CheckpointID
   211→	for _, info := range committed {
   212→		if strings.HasPrefix(info.CheckpointID.String(), checkpointIDPrefix) {
   213→			matches = append(matches, info.CheckpointID)
   214→		}
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	var fullCheckpointID id.CheckpointID
   218→	switch len(matches) {
   219→	case 0:
   220→		// Not found in committed, try temporary checkpoints by git SHA
   221→		if generate {
   222→			return fmt.Errorf("cannot generate summary for temporary checkpoint %s (only committed checkpoints supported)", checkpointIDPrefix)
   223→		}
   224→		output, found := explainTemporaryCheckpoint(w, repo, store, checkpointIDPrefix, verbose, full, rawTranscript)
   225→		if found {
   226→			outputExplainContent(w, output, noPager)
   227→			return nil
   228→		}
   229→		// If output is non-empty, it contains an error message (e.g., ambiguous prefix)
   230→		if output != "" {
   231→			return errors.New(output)
   232→		}
   233→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointIDPrefix)
   234→	case 1:
   235→		fullCheckpointID = matches[0]
   236→	default:
   237→		// Ambiguous prefix - show up to 5 examples
   238→		examples := make([]string, 0, 5)
   239→		for i := 0; i < len(matches) && i < 5; i++ {
   240→			examples = append(examples, matches[i].String())
   241→		}
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("ambiguous checkpoint prefix %q matches %d checkpoints: %s", checkpointIDPrefix, len(matches), strings.Join(examples, ", "))
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Load checkpoint summary
   246→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), fullCheckpointID)
   247→	if err != nil {
   248→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   249→	}
   250→	if summary == nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", fullCheckpointID)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Load latest session content (needed for transcript and metadata)
   255→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), fullCheckpointID)
   256→	if err != nil {
   257→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint content: %w", err)
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Handle summary generation
   261→	if generate {
   262→		if err := generateCheckpointSummary(w, errW, store, fullCheckpointID, summary, content, force); err != nil {
   263→			return err
   264→		}
   265→		// Reload the content to get the updated summary
   266→		content, err = store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), fullCheckpointID)
   267→		if err != nil {
   268→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to reload checkpoint: %w", err)
   269→		}
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// Handle raw transcript output
   273→	if rawTranscript {
   274→		if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   275→			return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint %s has no transcript", fullCheckpointID)
   276→		}
   277→		// Output raw transcript directly (no pager, no formatting)
   278→		if _, err = w.Write(content.Transcript); err != nil {
   279→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   280→		}
   281→		return nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	// Look up the author for this checkpoint (best-effort, ignore errors)
   285→	author, _ := store.GetCheckpointAuthor(context.Background(), fullCheckpointID) //nolint:errcheck // Author is optional
   286→
   287→	// Find associated commits (git commits with matching Entire-Checkpoint trailer)
   288→	associatedCommits, _ := getAssociatedCommits(repo, fullCheckpointID, searchAll) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   289→
   290→	// Format and output
   291→	output := formatCheckpointOutput(summary, content, fullCheckpointID, associatedCommits, author, verbose, full)
   292→	outputExplainContent(w, output, noPager)
   293→	return nil
   294→}
   295→
   296→// generateCheckpointSummary generates an AI summary for a checkpoint and persists it.
   297→// The summary is generated from the scoped transcript (only this checkpoint's portion),
   298→// not the entire session transcript.
   299→func generateCheckpointSummary(w, _ io.Writer, store *checkpoint.GitStore, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, cpSummary *checkpoint.CheckpointSummary, content *checkpoint.SessionContent, force bool) error {
   300→	// Check if summary already exists
   301→	if content.Metadata.Summary != nil && !force {
   302→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint %s already has a summary (use --force to regenerate)", checkpointID)
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	// Check if transcript exists
   306→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   307→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint %s has no transcript to summarize", checkpointID)
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	// Scope the transcript to only this checkpoint's portion
   311→	scopedTranscript := scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(content.Transcript, content.Metadata.GetTranscriptStart())
   312→	if len(scopedTranscript) == 0 {
   313→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint %s has no transcript content for this checkpoint (scoped)", checkpointID)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Generate summary using shared helper
   317→	ctx := context.Background()
   318→	logging.Info(ctx, "generating checkpoint summary")
   319→
   320→	summary, err := summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(ctx, scopedTranscript, cpSummary.FilesTouched, nil)
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate summary: %w", err)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Persist the summary
   326→	if err := store.UpdateSummary(ctx, checkpointID, summary); err != nil {
   327→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save summary: %w", err)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Summary generated and saved")
   331→	return nil
   332→}
   333→
   334→// explainTemporaryCheckpoint finds and formats a temporary checkpoint by shadow commit hash prefix.
   335→// Returns the formatted output and whether the checkpoint was found.
   336→// Searches ALL shadow branches, not just the one for current HEAD, to find checkpoints
   337→// created from different base commits (e.g., if HEAD advanced since session start).
   338→// The writer w is used for raw transcript output to bypass the pager.
   339→func explainTemporaryCheckpoint(w io.Writer, repo *git.Repository, store *checkpoint.GitStore, shaPrefix string, verbose, full, rawTranscript bool) (string, bool) {
   340→	// List temporary checkpoints from ALL shadow branches
   341→	// This ensures we find checkpoints even if HEAD has advanced since the session started
   342→	tempCheckpoints, err := store.ListAllTemporaryCheckpoints(context.Background(), "", branchCheckpointsLimit)
   343→	if err != nil {
   344→		return "", false
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Find checkpoints matching the SHA prefix - check for ambiguity
   348→	var matches []checkpoint.TemporaryCheckpointInfo
   349→	for _, tc := range tempCheckpoints {
   350→		if strings.HasPrefix(tc.CommitHash.String(), shaPrefix) {
   351→			matches = append(matches, tc)
   352→		}
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	if len(matches) == 0 {
   356→		return "", false
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   360→		// Multiple matches - return ambiguous error (consistent with committed checkpoint behavior)
   361→		var sb strings.Builder
   362→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "ambiguous checkpoint prefix %q matches %d temporary checkpoints:\n", shaPrefix, len(matches))
   363→		for _, m := range matches {
   364→			shortID := m.CommitHash.String()[:7]
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "  %s  %s  session %s\n",
   366→				shortID,
   367→				m.Timestamp.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"),
   368→				m.SessionID)
   369→		}
   370→		// Return as "not found" with error message - caller will use this as error
   371→		return sb.String(), false
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	tc := matches[0]
   375→
   376→	// Handle raw transcript output
   377→	if rawTranscript {
   378→		transcriptBytes, transcriptErr := store.GetTranscriptFromCommit(tc.CommitHash, tc.MetadataDir, agent.AgentTypeUnknown)
   379→		if transcriptErr != nil || len(transcriptBytes) == 0 {
   380→			// Return specific error message (consistent with committed checkpoints)
   381→			return fmt.Sprintf("checkpoint %s has no transcript", tc.CommitHash.String()[:7]), false
   382→		}
   383→		// Write directly to writer (no pager, no formatting) - matches committed checkpoint behavior
   384→		if _, writeErr := fmt.Fprint(w, string(transcriptBytes)); writeErr != nil {
   385→			return fmt.Sprintf("failed to write transcript: %v", writeErr), false
   386→		}
   387→		return "", true
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Found exactly one match - read metadata from shadow branch commit tree
   391→	shadowCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(tc.CommitHash)
   392→	if commitErr != nil {
   393→		return "", false
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	shadowTree, treeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   397→	if treeErr != nil {
   398→		return "", false
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Read prompts from shadow branch
   402→	sessionPrompt := strategy.ReadSessionPromptFromTree(shadowTree, tc.MetadataDir)
   403→
   404→	// Build output similar to formatCheckpointOutput but for temporary
   405→	var sb strings.Builder
   406→	shortID := tc.CommitHash.String()[:7]
   407→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Checkpoint: %s [temporary]\n", shortID)
   408→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session: %s\n", tc.SessionID)
   409→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Created: %s\n", tc.Timestamp.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   410→	sb.WriteString("\n")
   411→
   412→	// Intent from prompt
   413→	intent := "(not available)"
   414→	if sessionPrompt != "" {
   415→		lines := strings.Split(sessionPrompt, "\n")
   416→		if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
   417→			intent = strategy.TruncateDescription(lines[0], maxIntentDisplayLength)
   418→		}
   419→	}
   420→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Intent: %s\n", intent)
   421→	sb.WriteString("Outcome: (not generated)\n")
   422→
   423→	// Transcript section: full shows entire session, verbose shows checkpoint scope
   424→	// For temporary checkpoints, load transcript and compute scope from parent commit
   425→	var fullTranscript []byte
   426→	var scopedTranscript []byte
   427→	if full || verbose {
   428→		fullTranscript, _ = store.GetTranscriptFromCommit(tc.CommitHash, tc.MetadataDir, agent.AgentTypeUnknown) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   429→
   430→		if verbose && len(fullTranscript) > 0 {
   431→			// Compute scoped transcript by finding where parent's transcript ended
   432→			// Each shadow branch commit has the full transcript up to that point,
   433→			// so we diff against parent to get just this checkpoint's activity
   434→			scopedTranscript = fullTranscript // Default to full if no parent
   435→			if shadowCommit.NumParents() > 0 {
   436→				if parent, parentErr := shadowCommit.Parent(0); parentErr == nil {
   437→					parentTranscript, _ := store.GetTranscriptFromCommit(parent.Hash, tc.MetadataDir, agent.AgentTypeUnknown) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   438→					if len(parentTranscript) > 0 {
   439→						// Count lines in parent transcript to know where to slice from
   440→						parentLineCount := countLines(parentTranscript)
   441→						scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(fullTranscript, parentLineCount)
   442→					}
   443→				}
   444→			}
   445→		}
   446→	}
   447→	appendTranscriptSection(&sb, verbose, full, fullTranscript, scopedTranscript, sessionPrompt)
   448→
   449→	return sb.String(), true
   450→}
   451→
   452→// getAssociatedCommits finds git commits that reference the given checkpoint ID.
   453→// Searches commits on the current branch for Entire-Checkpoint trailer matches.
   454→// When searchAll is true, removes the commit scan limit (may be slow).
   455→func getAssociatedCommits(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, searchAll bool) ([]associatedCommit, error) {
   456→	head, err := repo.Head()
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	// Determine scan limit
   462→	limit := commitScanLimit
   463→	if searchAll {
   464→		limit = 0 // No limit
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	// Check if on default branch for filtering
   468→	isOnDefault, _ := strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)
   469→	var mainBranchHash plumbing.Hash
   470→	if !isOnDefault {
   471→		mainBranchHash = strategy.GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	// Precompute main branch commits for filtering (same logic as getBranchCheckpoints)
   475→	reachableFromMain := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)
   476→	if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   477→		mainIter, mainErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: mainBranchHash})
   478→		if mainErr == nil {
   479→			mainCount := 0
   480→			_ = mainIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort main branch detection
   481→				mainCount++
   482→				if mainCount > 1000 {
   483→					return errStopIteration
   484→				}
   485→				reachableFromMain[c.Hash] = true
   486→				return nil
   487→			})
   488→			mainIter.Close()
   489→		}
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   493→		From:  head.Hash(),
   494→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   495→	})
   496→	if err != nil {
   497→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   498→	}
   499→	defer iter.Close()
   500→
   501→	commits := []associatedCommit{} // Initialize as empty slice, not nil (nil means "not searched")
   502→	count := 0
   503→	targetID := checkpointID.String()
   504→
   505→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   506→		if limit > 0 && count >= limit {
   507→			return errStopIteration
   508→		}
   509→		count++
   510→
   511→		// Skip commits on main when on feature branch (same filtering as list view)
   512→		if len(reachableFromMain) > 0 && reachableFromMain[c.Hash] {
   513→			return nil
   514→		}
   515→
   516→		// Check for matching checkpoint trailer
   517→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   518→		if !found || cpID.String() != targetID {
   519→			return nil
   520→		}
   521→
   522→		fullSHA := c.Hash.String()
   523→		shortSHA := fullSHA
   524→		if len(fullSHA) >= 7 {
   525→			shortSHA = fullSHA[:7]
   526→		}
   527→
   528→		commits = append(commits, associatedCommit{
   529→			SHA:      fullSHA,
   530→			ShortSHA: shortSHA,
   531→			Message:  strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0],
   532→			Author:   c.Author.Name,
   533→			Date:     c.Author.When,
   534→		})
   535→
   536→		return nil
   537→	})
   538→
   539→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
   540→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("error iterating commits: %w", err)
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	return commits, nil
   544→}
   545→
   546→// scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint slices a transcript to include only the lines
   547→// relevant to a specific checkpoint, starting from linesAtStart.
   548→// This allows showing only what happened during a checkpoint, not the entire session.
   549→func scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(fullTranscript []byte, linesAtStart int) []byte {
   550→	return transcript.SliceFromLine(fullTranscript, linesAtStart)
   551→}
   552→
   553→// extractPromptsFromTranscript extracts user prompts from transcript bytes.
   554→// Returns a slice of prompt strings.
   555→func extractPromptsFromTranscript(transcriptBytes []byte) []string {
   556→	if len(transcriptBytes) == 0 {
   557→		return nil
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	condensed, err := summarize.BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes)
   561→	if err != nil {
   562→		return nil
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	var prompts []string
   566→	for _, entry := range condensed {
   567→		if entry.Type == summarize.EntryTypeUser && entry.Content != "" {
   568→			prompts = append(prompts, entry.Content)
   569→		}
   570→	}
   571→	return prompts
   572→}
   573→
   574→// formatCheckpointOutput formats checkpoint data based on verbosity level.
   575→// When verbose is false: summary only (ID, session, timestamp, tokens, intent).
   576→// When verbose is true: adds files, associated commits, and scoped transcript for this checkpoint.
   577→// When full is true: shows parsed full session transcript instead of scoped transcript.
   578→//
   579→// Transcript scope is controlled by CheckpointTranscriptStart in metadata, which indicates
   580→// where this checkpoint's content begins in the full session transcript.
   581→//
   582→// Author is displayed when available (only for committed checkpoints).
   583→// Associated commits are git commits that reference this checkpoint via Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   584→func formatCheckpointOutput(summary *checkpoint.CheckpointSummary, content *checkpoint.SessionContent, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, associatedCommits []associatedCommit, author checkpoint.Author, verbose, full bool) string {
   585→	var sb strings.Builder
   586→	meta := content.Metadata
   587→
   588→	// Scope the transcript to this checkpoint's portion
   589→	// If CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0, we slice the transcript to only include
   590→	// lines from that point onwards (excluding earlier checkpoint content)
   591→	scopedTranscript := scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(content.Transcript, meta.GetTranscriptStart())
   592→
   593→	// Extract prompts from the scoped transcript for intent extraction
   594→	scopedPrompts := extractPromptsFromTranscript(scopedTranscript)
   595→
   596→	// Header - always shown
   597→	// Note: CheckpointID is always exactly 12 characters, matching checkpointIDDisplayLength
   598→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Checkpoint: %s\n", checkpointID)
   599→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session: %s\n", meta.SessionID)
   600→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Created: %s\n", meta.CreatedAt.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   601→
   602→	// Author (only for committed checkpoints with known author)
   603→	if author.Name != "" {
   604→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Author: %s <%s>\n", author.Name, author.Email)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// Token usage - prefer content metadata, fall back to summary
   608→	tokenUsage := meta.TokenUsage
   609→	if tokenUsage == nil && summary != nil {
   610→		tokenUsage = summary.TokenUsage
   611→	}
   612→	if tokenUsage != nil {
   613→		totalTokens := tokenUsage.InputTokens + tokenUsage.CacheCreationTokens +
   614→			tokenUsage.CacheReadTokens + tokenUsage.OutputTokens
   615→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Tokens: %d\n", totalTokens)
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Associated commits section
   619→	if len(associatedCommits) > 0 {
   620→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   621→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commits: (%d)\n", len(associatedCommits))
   622→		for _, c := range associatedCommits {
   623→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "  %s %s %s\n", c.ShortSHA, c.Date.Format("2006-01-02"), c.Message)
   624→		}
   625→	} else if associatedCommits != nil {
   626→		// associatedCommits is non-nil but empty - show "no commits found" message
   627→		sb.WriteString("\nCommits: No commits found on this branch\n")
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	sb.WriteString("\n")
   631→
   632→	// Intent and Outcome from AI summary, or fallback to prompt text
   633→	if meta.Summary != nil {
   634→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Intent: %s\n", meta.Summary.Intent)
   635→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Outcome: %s\n", meta.Summary.Outcome)
   636→	} else {
   637→		// Fallback: use first line of scoped prompts for intent,
   638→		// or fall back to result.Prompts for backwards compatibility with older checkpoints
   639→		intent := "(not generated)"
   640→		if len(scopedPrompts) > 0 && scopedPrompts[0] != "" {
   641→			intent = strategy.TruncateDescription(scopedPrompts[0], maxIntentDisplayLength)
   642→		} else if content.Prompts != "" {
   643→			// Backwards compatibility: use stored prompts if no transcript available
   644→			lines := strings.Split(content.Prompts, "\n")
   645→			if len(lines) > 0 && lines[0] != "" {
   646→				intent = strategy.TruncateDescription(lines[0], maxIntentDisplayLength)
   647→			}
   648→		}
   649→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Intent: %s\n", intent)
   650→		sb.WriteString("Outcome: (not generated)\n")
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Verbose: add learnings, friction, files, and scoped transcript
   654→	if verbose || full {
   655→		// AI Summary details (learnings, friction, open items)
   656→		if meta.Summary != nil {
   657→			formatSummaryDetails(&sb, meta.Summary)
   658→		}
   659→
   660→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   661→
   662→		// Files section
   663→		if len(meta.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   664→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Files: (%d)\n", len(meta.FilesTouched))
   665→			for _, file := range meta.FilesTouched {
   666→				fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "  - %s\n", file)
   667→			}
   668→		} else {
   669→			sb.WriteString("Files: (none)\n")
   670→		}
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	// Transcript section: full shows entire session, verbose shows checkpoint scope
   674→	appendTranscriptSection(&sb, verbose, full, content.Transcript, scopedTranscript, content.Prompts)
   675→
   676→	return sb.String()
   677→}
   678→
   679→// appendTranscriptSection appends the appropriate transcript section to the builder
   680→// based on verbosity level. Full mode shows the entire session, verbose shows checkpoint scope.
   681→// fullTranscript is the entire session transcript, scopedContent is either scoped transcript bytes
   682→// or a pre-formatted string (for backwards compat), and scopedFallback is used when scoped parsing fails.
   683→func appendTranscriptSection(sb *strings.Builder, verbose, full bool, fullTranscript, scopedTranscript []byte, scopedFallback string) {
   684→	switch {
   685→	case full:
   686→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   687→		sb.WriteString("Transcript (full session):\n")
   688→		sb.WriteString(formatTranscriptBytes(fullTranscript, ""))
   689→
   690→	case verbose:
   691→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   692→		sb.WriteString("Transcript (checkpoint scope):\n")
   693→		sb.WriteString(formatTranscriptBytes(scopedTranscript, scopedFallback))
   694→	}
   695→}
   696→
   697→// formatTranscriptBytes formats transcript bytes into a human-readable string.
   698→// It parses the JSONL transcript and formats it using the condensed format.
   699→// The fallback is used for backwards compatibility when transcript parsing fails or is empty.
   700→func formatTranscriptBytes(transcriptBytes []byte, fallback string) string {
   701→	if len(transcriptBytes) == 0 {
   702→		if fallback != "" {
   703→			return fallback + "\n"
   704→		}
   705→		return "  (none)\n"
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	condensed, err := summarize.BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes)
   709→	if err != nil || len(condensed) == 0 {
   710→		if fallback != "" {
   711→			return fallback + "\n"
   712→		}
   713→		return "  (failed to parse transcript)\n"
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	input := summarize.Input{Transcript: condensed}
   717→	return summarize.FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
   718→}
   719→
   720→// formatSummaryDetails formats the detailed sections of an AI summary.
   721→func formatSummaryDetails(sb *strings.Builder, summary *checkpoint.Summary) {
   722→	// Learnings section
   723→	hasLearnings := len(summary.Learnings.Repo) > 0 ||
   724→		len(summary.Learnings.Code) > 0 ||
   725→		len(summary.Learnings.Workflow) > 0
   726→
   727→	if hasLearnings {
   728→		sb.WriteString("\nLearnings:\n")
   729→
   730→		if len(summary.Learnings.Repo) > 0 {
   731→			sb.WriteString("  Repository:\n")
   732→			for _, learning := range summary.Learnings.Repo {
   733→				fmt.Fprintf(sb, "    - %s\n", learning)
   734→			}
   735→		}
   736→
   737→		if len(summary.Learnings.Code) > 0 {
   738→			sb.WriteString("  Code:\n")
   739→			for _, learning := range summary.Learnings.Code {
   740→				if learning.Line > 0 {
   741→					if learning.EndLine > 0 {
   742→						fmt.Fprintf(sb, "    - %s:%d-%d: %s\n", learning.Path, learning.Line, learning.EndLine, learning.Finding)
   743→					} else {
   744→						fmt.Fprintf(sb, "    - %s:%d: %s\n", learning.Path, learning.Line, learning.Finding)
   745→					}
   746→				} else {
   747→					fmt.Fprintf(sb, "    - %s: %s\n", learning.Path, learning.Finding)
   748→				}
   749→			}
   750→		}
   751→
   752→		if len(summary.Learnings.Workflow) > 0 {
   753→			sb.WriteString("  Workflow:\n")
   754→			for _, learning := range summary.Learnings.Workflow {
   755→				fmt.Fprintf(sb, "    - %s\n", learning)
   756→			}
   757→		}
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	// Friction section
   761→	if len(summary.Friction) > 0 {
   762→		sb.WriteString("\nFriction:\n")
   763→		for _, item := range summary.Friction {
   764→			fmt.Fprintf(sb, "  - %s\n", item)
   765→		}
   766→	}
   767→
   768→	// Open items section
   769→	if len(summary.OpenItems) > 0 {
   770→		sb.WriteString("\nOpen Items:\n")
   771→		for _, item := range summary.OpenItems {
   772→			fmt.Fprintf(sb, "  - %s\n", item)
   773→		}
   774→	}
   775→}
   776→
   777→// runExplainDefault shows all checkpoints on the current branch.
   778→// This is the default view when no flags are provided.
   779→func runExplainDefault(w io.Writer, noPager bool) error {
   780→	return runExplainBranchDefault(w, noPager)
   781→}
   782→
   783→// branchCheckpointsLimit is the max checkpoints to show in branch view
   784→const branchCheckpointsLimit = 100
   785→
   786→// commitScanLimit is how far back to scan git history for checkpoints
   787→const commitScanLimit = 500
   788→
   789→// consecutiveMainLimit stops scanning after this many consecutive commits on main
   790→// (indicates we've likely exhausted feature branch commits)
   791→const consecutiveMainLimit = 100
   792→
   793→// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early
   794→var errStopIteration = errors.New("stop iteration")
   795→
   796→// getBranchCheckpoints returns checkpoints relevant to the current branch.
   797→// This is strategy-agnostic - it queries checkpoints directly from the checkpoint store.
   798→//
   799→// Behavior:
   800→//   - On feature branches: only show checkpoints unique to this branch (not in main)
   801→//   - On default branch (main/master): show all checkpoints in history (up to limit)
   802→//   - Includes both committed checkpoints (entire/checkpoints/v1) and temporary checkpoints (shadow branches)
   803→func getBranchCheckpoints(repo *git.Repository, limit int) ([]strategy.RewindPoint, error) {
   804→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   805→
   806→	// Get all committed checkpoints for lookup
   807→	committedInfos, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   808→	if err != nil {
   809→		committedInfos = nil // Continue without committed checkpoints
   810→	}
   811→
   812→	// Build map of checkpoint ID -> committed info
   813→	committedByID := make(map[id.CheckpointID]checkpoint.CommittedInfo)
   814→	for _, info := range committedInfos {
   815→		if !info.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   816→			committedByID[info.CheckpointID] = info
   817→		}
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	head, err := repo.Head()
   821→	if err != nil {
   822→		// Unborn HEAD (no commits yet) - return empty list instead of erroring
   823→		if errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound) {
   824→			return []strategy.RewindPoint{}, nil
   825→		}
   826→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	// Check if we're on the default branch (use repo-aware version)
   830→	isOnDefault, _ := strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)
   831→	var mainBranchHash plumbing.Hash
   832→	if !isOnDefault {
   833→		// Prefer the actual default branch name (which may not be main/master)
   834→		if defaultBranchName := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo); defaultBranchName != "" {
   835→			// Try local default branch first: refs/heads/<name>
   836→			ref, refErr := repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+defaultBranchName), true)
   837→			if refErr != nil {
   838→				// Fall back to remote default branch: refs/remotes/origin/<name>
   839→				ref, refErr = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/remotes/origin/"+defaultBranchName), true)
   840→			}
   841→			if refErr == nil {
   842→				mainBranchHash = ref.Hash()
   843→			}
   844→		}
   845→
   846→		// Fall back to main/master detection if default branch resolution failed
   847→		if mainBranchHash == plumbing.ZeroHash {
   848→			mainBranchHash = strategy.GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   849→		}
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Precompute commits reachable from main (for O(1) filtering instead of O(N) per commit)
   853→	// This changes the filtering from O(N*M) to O(N+M) where N=commits scanned, M=main history depth
   854→	reachableFromMain := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)
   855→	if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   856→		mainIter, mainErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: mainBranchHash})
   857→		if mainErr == nil {
   858→			mainCount := 0
   859→			_ = mainIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   860→				mainCount++
   861→				if mainCount > 1000 { // Same depth limit as before
   862→					return errStopIteration
   863→				}
   864→				reachableFromMain[c.Hash] = true
   865→				return nil
   866→			})
   867→			mainIter.Close()
   868→		}
   869→	}
   870→
   871→	// Walk git history and collect checkpoints
   872→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   873→		From:  head.Hash(),
   874→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   875→	})
   876→	if err != nil {
   877→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   878→	}
   879→	defer iter.Close()
   880→
   881→	var points []strategy.RewindPoint
   882→	count := 0
   883→	consecutiveMainCount := 0
   884→
   885→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   886→		if count >= commitScanLimit {
   887→			return errStopIteration
   888→		}
   889→		count++
   890→
   891→		// On feature branches, skip commits that are reachable from main
   892→		// (but continue scanning - there may be more feature branch commits)
   893→		if len(reachableFromMain) > 0 {
   894→			if reachableFromMain[c.Hash] {
   895→				consecutiveMainCount++
   896→				if consecutiveMainCount >= consecutiveMainLimit {
   897→					return errStopIteration // Likely exhausted feature branch commits
   898→				}
   899→				return nil // Skip this commit, continue scanning
   900→			}
   901→			consecutiveMainCount = 0 // Reset on feature branch commit
   902→		}
   903→
   904→		// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   905→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   906→		if !found {
   907→			return nil // No checkpoint trailer, continue
   908→		}
   909→
   910→		// Look up checkpoint info
   911→		cpInfo, found := committedByID[cpID]
   912→		if !found {
   913→			return nil // Checkpoint not in store, continue
   914→		}
   915→
   916→		// Create rewind point from committed info
   917→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   918→		point := strategy.RewindPoint{
   919→			ID:               c.Hash.String(),
   920→			Message:          message,
   921→			Date:             c.Committer.When,
   922→			IsLogsOnly:       true, // Committed checkpoints are logs-only
   923→			CheckpointID:     cpID,
   924→			SessionID:        cpInfo.SessionID,
   925→			IsTaskCheckpoint: cpInfo.IsTask,
   926→			ToolUseID:        cpInfo.ToolUseID,
   927→			Agent:            cpInfo.Agent,
   928→		}
   929→		// Read session prompt from metadata branch (best-effort)
   930→		content, _ := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID) //nolint:errcheck  // Best-effort
   931→		if content != nil {
   932→			// Scope the transcript to this checkpoint's portion
   933→			// If CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0, we slice the transcript to only include
   934→			// lines from that point onwards (excluding earlier checkpoint content)
   935→			scopedTranscript := scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(content.Transcript, content.Metadata.GetTranscriptStart())
   936→			// Extract prompts from the scoped transcript (not the full session's prompts)
   937→			scopedPrompts := extractPromptsFromTranscript(scopedTranscript)
   938→			if len(scopedPrompts) > 0 && scopedPrompts[0] != "" {
   939→				point.SessionPrompt = scopedPrompts[0]
   940→			}
   941→		}
   942→
   943→		points = append(points, point)
   944→		return nil
   945→	})
   946→
   947→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
   948→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("error iterating commits: %w", err)
   949→	}
   950→
   951→	// Get temporary checkpoints from ALL shadow branches whose base commit is reachable from HEAD.
   952→	tempPoints := getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints(repo, store, head.Hash(), isOnDefault, limit)
   953→	points = append(points, tempPoints...)
   954→
   955→	// Sort by date, most recent first
   956→	sort.Slice(points, func(i, j int) bool {
   957→		return points[i].Date.After(points[j].Date)
   958→	})
   959→
   960→	// Apply limit
   961→	if len(points) > limit {
   962→		points = points[:limit]
   963→	}
   964→
   965→	return points, nil
   966→}
   967→
   968→// getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints returns temporary checkpoints from shadow branches
   969→// whose base commit is reachable from the given HEAD hash.
   970→// For default branches, all shadow branches are included.
   971→// For feature branches, only shadow branches whose base commit is in HEAD's history are included.
   972→func getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints(repo *git.Repository, store *checkpoint.GitStore, headHash plumbing.Hash, isOnDefault bool, limit int) []strategy.RewindPoint {
   973→	var points []strategy.RewindPoint
   974→
   975→	shadowBranches, _ := store.ListTemporary(context.Background()) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   976→	for _, sb := range shadowBranches {
   977→		// Check if this shadow branch's base commit is reachable from current HEAD
   978→		if !isShadowBranchReachable(repo, sb.BaseCommit, headHash, isOnDefault) {
   979→			continue
   980→		}
   981→
   982→		// List checkpoints from this shadow branch
   983→		tempCheckpoints, _ := store.ListCheckpointsForBranch(context.Background(), sb.BranchName, "", limit) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
   984→		for _, tc := range tempCheckpoints {
   985→			point := convertTemporaryCheckpoint(repo, tc)
   986→			if point != nil {
   987→				points = append(points, *point)
   988→			}
   989→		}
   990→	}
   991→
   992→	return points
   993→}
   994→
   995→// isShadowBranchReachable checks if a shadow branch's base commit is reachable from HEAD.
   996→// For default branches, all shadow branches are considered reachable.
   997→// For feature branches, we check if any commit with the base commit prefix is in HEAD's history.
   998→func isShadowBranchReachable(repo *git.Repository, baseCommit string, headHash plumbing.Hash, isOnDefault bool) bool {
   999→	// For default branch: all shadow branches are potentially relevant
  1000→	if isOnDefault {
  1001→		return true
  1002→	}
  1003→
  1004→	// Check if base commit hash prefix matches any commit in HEAD's history
  1005→	baseCommitIter, baseErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: headHash})
  1006→	if baseErr != nil {
  1007→		return false
  1008→	}
  1009→	defer baseCommitIter.Close()
  1010→
  1011→	baseCount := 0
  1012→	found := false
  1013→	_ = baseCommitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort
  1014→		baseCount++
  1015→		if baseCount > commitScanLimit {
  1016→			return errStopIteration
  1017→		}
  1018→		if strings.HasPrefix(c.Hash.String(), baseCommit) {
  1019→			found = true
  1020→			return errStopIteration
  1021→		}
  1022→		return nil
  1023→	})
  1024→
  1025→	return found
  1026→}
  1027→
  1028→// convertTemporaryCheckpoint converts a TemporaryCheckpointInfo to a RewindPoint.
  1029→// Returns nil if the checkpoint should be skipped (no code changes or can't be read).
  1030→func convertTemporaryCheckpoint(repo *git.Repository, tc checkpoint.TemporaryCheckpointInfo) *strategy.RewindPoint {
  1031→	shadowCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(tc.CommitHash)
  1032→	if commitErr != nil {
  1033→		return nil
  1034→	}
  1035→
  1036→	// Filter out checkpoints with no code changes (only .entire/ metadata changed)
  1037→	// This also filters out the first checkpoint which is just a baseline copy
  1038→	if !hasCodeChanges(shadowCommit) {
  1039→		return nil
  1040→	}
  1041→
  1042→	// Read session prompt from the shadow branch commit's tree (not from entire/checkpoints/v1)
  1043→	// Temporary checkpoints store their metadata in the shadow branch, not in entire/checkpoints/v1
  1044→	var sessionPrompt string
  1045→	shadowTree, treeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
  1046→	if treeErr == nil {
  1047→		sessionPrompt = strategy.ReadSessionPromptFromTree(shadowTree, tc.MetadataDir)
  1048→	}
  1049→
  1050→	return &strategy.RewindPoint{
  1051→		ID:               tc.CommitHash.String(),
  1052→		Message:          tc.Message,
  1053→		MetadataDir:      tc.MetadataDir,
  1054→		Date:             tc.Timestamp,
  1055→		IsTaskCheckpoint: tc.IsTaskCheckpoint,
  1056→		ToolUseID:        tc.ToolUseID,
  1057→		SessionID:        tc.SessionID,
  1058→		SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
  1059→		IsLogsOnly:       false, // Temporary checkpoints can be fully rewound
  1060→	}
  1061→}
  1062→
  1063→// runExplainBranchWithFilter shows checkpoints on the current branch, optionally filtered by session.
  1064→// This is strategy-agnostic - it queries checkpoints directly.
  1065→func runExplainBranchWithFilter(w io.Writer, noPager bool, sessionFilter string) error {
  1066→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1067→	if err != nil {
  1068→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
  1069→	}
  1070→
  1071→	// Get current branch name
  1072→	branchName := strategy.GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
  1073→	if branchName == "" {
  1074→		// Detached HEAD state or unborn HEAD - try to use short commit hash if possible
  1075→		head, headErr := repo.Head()
  1076→		if headErr != nil {
  1077→			// Unborn HEAD (no commits yet) - treat as empty history instead of erroring
  1078→			if errors.Is(headErr, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound) {
  1079→				branchName = "HEAD (no commits yet)"
  1080→			} else {
  1081→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", headErr)
  1082→			}
  1083→		} else {
  1084→			branchName = "HEAD (" + head.Hash().String()[:7] + ")"
  1085→		}
  1086→	}
  1087→
  1088→	// Get checkpoints for this branch (strategy-agnostic)
  1089→	points, err := getBranchCheckpoints(repo, branchCheckpointsLimit)
  1090→	if err != nil {
  1091→		// Log the error but continue with empty list so user sees helpful message
  1092→		logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to get branch checkpoints", "error", err)
  1093→		points = nil
  1094→	}
  1095→
  1096→	// Format output
  1097→	output := formatBranchCheckpoints(branchName, points, sessionFilter)
  1098→
  1099→	outputExplainContent(w, output, noPager)
  1100→	return nil
  1101→}
  1102→
  1103→// runExplainBranchDefault shows all checkpoints on the current branch grouped by date.
  1104→// This is a convenience wrapper that calls runExplainBranchWithFilter with no filter.
  1105→func runExplainBranchDefault(w io.Writer, noPager bool) error {
  1106→	return runExplainBranchWithFilter(w, noPager, "")
  1107→}
  1108→
  1109→// outputExplainContent outputs content with optional pager support.
  1110→func outputExplainContent(w io.Writer, content string, noPager bool) {
  1111→	if noPager {
  1112→		fmt.Fprint(w, content)
  1113→	} else {
  1114→		outputWithPager(w, content)
  1115→	}
  1116→}
  1117→
  1118→// runExplainCommit looks up the checkpoint associated with a commit.
  1119→// Extracts the Entire-Checkpoint trailer and delegates to checkpoint detail view.
  1120→// If no trailer found, shows a message indicating no associated checkpoint.
  1121→func runExplainCommit(w io.Writer, commitRef string, noPager, verbose, full, searchAll bool) error {
  1122→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1123→	if err != nil {
  1124→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
  1125→	}
  1126→
  1127→	// Resolve the commit reference
  1128→	hash, err := repo.ResolveRevision(plumbing.Revision(commitRef))
  1129→	if err != nil {
  1130→		return fmt.Errorf("commit not found: %s", commitRef)
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(*hash)
  1134→	if err != nil {
  1135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1136→	}
  1137→
  1138→	// Extract Entire-Checkpoint trailer
  1139→	checkpointID, hasCheckpoint := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
  1140→	if !hasCheckpoint {
  1141→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No associated Entire checkpoint")
  1142→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nCommit %s does not have an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.\n", hash.String()[:7])
  1143→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "This commit was not created during an Entire session, or the trailer was removed.")
  1144→		return nil
  1145→	}
  1146→
  1147→	// Delegate to checkpoint detail view
  1148→	// Note: errW is only used for generate mode, but we pass w for safety
  1149→	return runExplainCheckpoint(w, w, checkpointID.String(), noPager, verbose, full, false, false, false, searchAll)
  1150→}
  1151→
  1152→// formatSessionInfo formats session information for display.
  1153→func formatSessionInfo(session *strategy.Session, sourceRef string, checkpoints []checkpointDetail) string {
  1154→	var sb strings.Builder
  1155→
  1156→	// Session header
  1157→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session: %s\n", session.ID)
  1158→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Strategy: %s\n", session.Strategy)
  1159→
  1160→	if !session.StartTime.IsZero() {
  1161→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Started: %s\n", session.StartTime.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
  1162→	}
  1163→
  1164→	if sourceRef != "" {
  1165→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Source Ref: %s\n", sourceRef)
  1166→	}
  1167→
  1168→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Checkpoints: %d\n", len(checkpoints))
  1169→
  1170→	// Checkpoint details
  1171→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
  1172→		sb.WriteString("\n")
  1173→
  1174→		// Checkpoint header
  1175→		taskMarker := ""
  1176→		if cp.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1177→			taskMarker = " [Task]"
  1178→		}
  1179→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "─── Checkpoint %d [%s] %s%s ───\n",
  1180→			cp.Index, cp.ShortID, cp.Timestamp.Format("2006-01-02 15:04"), taskMarker)
  1181→		sb.WriteString("\n")
  1182→
  1183→		// Display all interactions in this checkpoint
  1184→		for i, inter := range cp.Interactions {
  1185→			// For multiple interactions, add a sub-header
  1186→			if len(cp.Interactions) > 1 {
  1187→				fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Interaction %d\n\n", i+1)
  1188→			}
  1189→
  1190→			// Prompt section
  1191→			if inter.Prompt != "" {
  1192→				sb.WriteString("## Prompt\n\n")
  1193→				sb.WriteString(inter.Prompt)
  1194→				sb.WriteString("\n\n")
  1195→			}
  1196→
  1197→			// Response section
  1198→			if len(inter.Responses) > 0 {
  1199→				sb.WriteString("## Responses\n\n")
  1200→				sb.WriteString(strings.Join(inter.Responses, "\n\n"))
  1201→				sb.WriteString("\n\n")
  1202→			}
  1203→
  1204→			// Files modified for this interaction
  1205→			if len(inter.Files) > 0 {
  1206→				fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Files Modified (%d):\n", len(inter.Files))
  1207→				for _, file := range inter.Files {
  1208→					fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "  - %s\n", file)
  1209→				}
  1210→				sb.WriteString("\n")
  1211→			}
  1212→		}
  1213→
  1214→		// If no interactions, show message and/or files
  1215→		if len(cp.Interactions) == 0 {
  1216→			// Show commit message as summary when no transcript available
  1217→			if cp.Message != "" {
  1218→				sb.WriteString(cp.Message)
  1219→				sb.WriteString("\n\n")
  1220→			}
  1221→			// Show aggregate files if available
  1222→			if len(cp.Files) > 0 {
  1223→				fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Files Modified (%d):\n", len(cp.Files))
  1224→				for _, file := range cp.Files {
  1225→					fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "  - %s\n", file)
  1226→				}
  1227→			}
  1228→		}
  1229→	}
  1230→
  1231→	return sb.String()
  1232→}
  1233→
  1234→// outputWithPager outputs content through a pager if stdout is a terminal and content is long.
  1235→func outputWithPager(w io.Writer, content string) {
  1236→	// Check if we're writing to stdout and it's a terminal
  1237→	if f, ok := w.(*os.File); ok && f == os.Stdout && term.IsTerminal(int(f.Fd())) {
  1238→		// Get terminal height
  1239→		_, height, err := term.GetSize(int(f.Fd()))
  1240→		if err != nil {
  1241→			height = 24 // Default fallback
  1242→		}
  1243→
  1244→		// Count lines in content
  1245→		lineCount := strings.Count(content, "\n")
  1246→
  1247→		// Use pager if content exceeds terminal height
  1248→		if lineCount > height-2 {
  1249→			pager := os.Getenv("PAGER")
  1250→			if pager == "" {
  1251→				pager = "less"
  1252→			}
  1253→
  1254→			cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), pager) //nolint:gosec // pager from env is expected
  1255→			cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(content)
  1256→			cmd.Stdout = f
  1257→			cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
  1258→
  1259→			if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
  1260→				// Fallback to direct output if pager fails
  1261→				fmt.Fprint(w, content)
  1262→			}
  1263→			return
  1264→		}
  1265→	}
  1266→
  1267→	// Direct output for non-terminal or short content
  1268→	fmt.Fprint(w, content)
  1269→}
  1270→
  1271→// Constants for formatting output
  1272→const (
  1273→	// maxIntentDisplayLength is the maximum length for intent text before truncation
  1274→	maxIntentDisplayLength = 80
  1275→	// maxMessageDisplayLength is the maximum length for checkpoint messages before truncation
  1276→	maxMessageDisplayLength = 80
  1277→	// maxPromptDisplayLength is the maximum length for session prompts before truncation
  1278→	maxPromptDisplayLength = 60
  1279→	// checkpointIDDisplayLength is the number of characters to show from checkpoint IDs
  1280→	checkpointIDDisplayLength = 12
  1281→)
  1282→
  1283→// formatBranchCheckpoints formats checkpoint information for a branch.
  1284→// Groups commits by checkpoint ID and shows the prompt for each checkpoint.
  1285→// If sessionFilter is non-empty, only shows checkpoints matching that session ID (or prefix).
  1286→func formatBranchCheckpoints(branchName string, points []strategy.RewindPoint, sessionFilter string) string {
  1287→	var sb strings.Builder
  1288→
  1289→	// Branch header
  1290→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Branch: %s\n", branchName)
  1291→
  1292→	// Filter by session if specified
  1293→	if sessionFilter != "" {
  1294→		var filtered []strategy.RewindPoint
  1295→		for _, p := range points {
  1296→			if p.SessionID == sessionFilter || strings.HasPrefix(p.SessionID, sessionFilter) {
  1297→				filtered = append(filtered, p)
  1298→			}
  1299→		}
  1300→		points = filtered
  1301→	}
  1302→
  1303→	if len(points) == 0 {
  1304→		sb.WriteString("Checkpoints: 0\n")
  1305→		if sessionFilter != "" {
  1306→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Filtered by session: %s\n", sessionFilter)
  1307→		}
  1308→		sb.WriteString("\nNo checkpoints found on this branch.\n")
  1309→		sb.WriteString("Checkpoints will appear here after you save changes during a Claude session.\n")
  1310→		return sb.String()
  1311→	}
  1312→
  1313→	// Group by checkpoint ID
  1314→	groups := groupByCheckpointID(points)
  1315→
  1316→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Checkpoints: %d\n", len(groups))
  1317→	if sessionFilter != "" {
  1318→		fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Filtered by session: %s\n", sessionFilter)
  1319→	}
  1320→	sb.WriteString("\n")
  1321→
  1322→	// Output each checkpoint group
  1323→	for _, group := range groups {
  1324→		formatCheckpointGroup(&sb, group)
  1325→		sb.WriteString("\n")
  1326→	}
  1327→
  1328→	return sb.String()
  1329→}
  1330→
  1331→// checkpointGroup represents a group of commits sharing the same checkpoint ID.
  1332→type checkpointGroup struct {
  1333→	checkpointID string
  1334→	prompt       string
  1335→	isTemporary  bool // true if any commit is not logs-only (can be rewound)
  1336→	isTask       bool // true if this is a task checkpoint
  1337→	commits      []commitEntry
  1338→}
  1339→
  1340→// commitEntry represents a single git commit within a checkpoint.
  1341→type commitEntry struct {
  1342→	date    time.Time
  1343→	gitSHA  string // short git SHA
  1344→	message string
  1345→}
  1346→
  1347→// groupByCheckpointID groups rewind points by their checkpoint ID.
  1348→// Returns groups sorted by latest commit timestamp (most recent first).
  1349→func groupByCheckpointID(points []strategy.RewindPoint) []checkpointGroup {
  1350→	if len(points) == 0 {
  1351→		return nil
  1352→	}
  1353→
  1354→	// Build map of checkpoint ID -> group
  1355→	groupMap := make(map[string]*checkpointGroup)
  1356→	var order []string // Track insertion order for stable iteration
  1357→
  1358→	for _, point := range points {
  1359→		// Determine the checkpoint ID to use for grouping
  1360→		cpID := point.CheckpointID.String()
  1361→		if cpID == "" {
  1362→			// Temporary checkpoints: group by session ID to preserve per-session prompts
  1363→			// Use session ID prefix for readability (format: YYYY-MM-DD-uuid)
  1364→			cpID = point.SessionID
  1365→			if cpID == "" {
  1366→				cpID = "temporary" // Fallback if no session ID
  1367→			}
  1368→		}
  1369→
  1370→		group, exists := groupMap[cpID]
  1371→		if !exists {
  1372→			group = &checkpointGroup{
  1373→				checkpointID: cpID,
  1374→				prompt:       point.SessionPrompt,
  1375→				isTemporary:  !point.IsLogsOnly,
  1376→				isTask:       point.IsTaskCheckpoint,
  1377→			}
  1378→			groupMap[cpID] = group
  1379→			order = append(order, cpID)
  1380→		}
  1381→
  1382→		// Short git SHA (7 chars)
  1383→		gitSHA := point.ID
  1384→		if len(gitSHA) > 7 {
  1385→			gitSHA = gitSHA[:7]
  1386→		}
  1387→
  1388→		group.commits = append(group.commits, commitEntry{
  1389→			date:    point.Date,
  1390→			gitSHA:  gitSHA,
  1391→			message: point.Message,
  1392→		})
  1393→
  1394→		// Update flags - if any commit is temporary/task, the group is too
  1395→		if !point.IsLogsOnly {
  1396→			group.isTemporary = true
  1397→		}
  1398→		if point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
  1399→			group.isTask = true
  1400→		}
  1401→		// Update prompt if the group's prompt is empty but this point has one
  1402→		if group.prompt == "" && point.SessionPrompt != "" {
  1403→			group.prompt = point.SessionPrompt
  1404→		}
  1405→	}
  1406→
  1407→	// Sort commits within each group by date (most recent first)
  1408→	for _, group := range groupMap {
  1409→		sort.Slice(group.commits, func(i, j int) bool {
  1410→			return group.commits[i].date.After(group.commits[j].date)
  1411→		})
  1412→	}
  1413→
  1414→	// Build result slice in order, then sort by latest commit
  1415→	result := make([]checkpointGroup, 0, len(order))
  1416→	for _, cpID := range order {
  1417→		result = append(result, *groupMap[cpID])
  1418→	}
  1419→
  1420→	// Sort groups by latest commit timestamp (most recent first)
  1421→	sort.Slice(result, func(i, j int) bool {
  1422→		// Each group's commits are already sorted, so first commit is latest
  1423→		if len(result[i].commits) == 0 {
  1424→			return false
  1425→		}
  1426→		if len(result[j].commits) == 0 {
  1427→			return true
  1428→		}
  1429→		return result[i].commits[0].date.After(result[j].commits[0].date)
  1430→	})
  1431→
  1432→	return result
  1433→}
  1434→
  1435→// formatCheckpointGroup formats a single checkpoint group for display.
  1436→func formatCheckpointGroup(sb *strings.Builder, group checkpointGroup) {
  1437→	// Checkpoint ID (truncated for display)
  1438→	cpID := group.checkpointID
  1439→	if len(cpID) > checkpointIDDisplayLength {
  1440→		cpID = cpID[:checkpointIDDisplayLength]
  1441→	}
  1442→
  1443→	// Build status indicators
  1444→	// Skip [temporary] indicator when cpID is already "temporary" to avoid redundancy
  1445→	var indicators []string
  1446→	if group.isTask {
  1447→		indicators = append(indicators, "[Task]")
  1448→	}
  1449→	if group.isTemporary && cpID != "temporary" {
  1450→		indicators = append(indicators, "[temporary]")
  1451→	}
  1452→
  1453→	indicatorStr := ""
  1454→	if len(indicators) > 0 {
  1455→		indicatorStr = " " + strings.Join(indicators, " ")
  1456→	}
  1457→
  1458→	// Prompt (truncated)
  1459→	var promptStr string
  1460→	if group.prompt == "" {
  1461→		promptStr = "(no prompt)"
  1462→	} else {
  1463→		// Quote actual prompts
  1464→		promptStr = fmt.Sprintf("%q", strategy.TruncateDescription(group.prompt, maxPromptDisplayLength))
  1465→	}
  1466→
  1467→	// Checkpoint header: [checkpoint_id] [indicators] prompt
  1468→	fmt.Fprintf(sb, "[%s]%s %s\n", cpID, indicatorStr, promptStr)
  1469→
  1470→	// List commits under this checkpoint
  1471→	for _, commit := range group.commits {
  1472→		// Format: "  MM-DD HH:MM (git_sha) message"
  1473→		dateTimeStr := commit.date.Format("01-02 15:04")
  1474→		message := strategy.TruncateDescription(commit.message, maxMessageDisplayLength)
  1475→		fmt.Fprintf(sb, "  %s (%s) %s\n", dateTimeStr, commit.gitSHA, message)
  1476→	}
  1477→}
  1478→
  1479→// countLines counts the number of lines in a byte slice.
  1480→// For JSONL content (where each line ends with \n), this returns the line count.
  1481→// Empty content returns 0.
  1482→func countLines(content []byte) int {
  1483→	if len(content) == 0 {
  1484→		return 0
  1485→	}
  1486→	count := 0
  1487→	for _, b := range content {
  1488→		if b == '\n' {
  1489→			count++
  1490→		}
  1491→	}
  1492→	return count
  1493→}
  1494→
  1495→// hasCodeChanges returns true if the commit has changes to non-metadata files.
  1496→// Used by getBranchCheckpoints to filter out metadata-only temporary checkpoints.
  1497→// Returns false only if the commit has a parent AND only modified .entire/ metadata files.
  1498→//
  1499→// First commits (no parent) are always considered to have code changes since they
  1500→// capture the working copy state at session start - real uncommitted work.
  1501→//
  1502→// This filters out periodic transcript saves that don't change code.
  1503→func hasCodeChanges(commit *object.Commit) bool {
  1504→	// First commit on shadow branch captures working copy state - always meaningful
  1505→	if commit.NumParents() == 0 {
  1506→		return true
  1507→	}
  1508→
  1509→	parent, err := commit.Parent(0)
  1510→	if err != nil {
  1511→		return true // Can't check, assume meaningful
  1512→	}
  1513→
  1514→	commitTree, err := commit.Tree()
  1515→	if err != nil {
  1516→		return true
  1517→	}
  1518→
  1519→	parentTree, err := parent.Tree()
  1520→	if err != nil {
  1521→		return true
  1522→	}
  1523→
  1524→	changes, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)
  1525→	if err != nil {
  1526→		return true
  1527→	}
  1528→
  1529→	// Check if any non-metadata file was changed
  1530→	for _, change := range changes {
  1531→		name := change.To.Name
  1532→		if name == "" {
  1533→			name = change.From.Name
  1534→		}
  1535→		// Skip .entire/ metadata files
  1536→		if !strings.HasPrefix(name, ".entire/") {
  1537→			return true
  1538→		}
  1539→	}
  1540→
  1541→	return false
  1542→}
  1543→

  1720→func TestHasCodeChanges_FirstCommitReturnsTrue(t *testing.T) {
  1721→	// First commit on a shadow branch (no parent) should return true
  1722→	// since it captures the working copy state - real uncommitted work
  1723→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
  1724→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
  1725→
  1726→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(tmpDir, false)
  1727→	if err != nil {
  1728→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
  1729→	}
  1730→
  1731→	w, err := repo.Worktree()
  1732→	if err != nil {
  1733→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
  1734→	}
  1735→
  1736→	// Create first commit (has no parent)
  1737→	testFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "test.txt")
  1738→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("initial"), 0o644); err != nil {
  1739→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
  1740→	}
  1741→	if _, err := w.Add("test.txt"); err != nil {
  1742→		t.Fatalf("failed to add test file: %v", err)
  1743→	}
  1744→	commitHash, err := w.Commit("first commit", &git.CommitOptions{
  1745→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@example.com", When: time.Now()},
  1746→	})
  1747→	if err != nil {
  1748→		t.Fatalf("failed to create commit: %v", err)
  1749→	}
  1750→
  1751→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
  1752→	if err != nil {
  1753→		t.Fatalf("failed to get commit object: %v", err)
  1754→	}
  1755→
  1756→	// First commit (no parent) captures working copy state - should return true
  1757→	if !hasCodeChanges(commit) {
  1758→		t.Error("hasCodeChanges() should return true for first commit (captures working copy)")
  1759→	}
  1760→}

  1938→func TestGetBranchCheckpoints_FiltersMainCommits(t *testing.T) {
  1939→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
  1940→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
  1941→
  1942→	// Initialize git repo
  1943→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(tmpDir, false)
  1944→	if err != nil {
  1945→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
  1946→	}
  1947→
  1948→	w, err := repo.Worktree()
  1949→	if err != nil {
  1950→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
  1951→	}
  1952→
  1953→	// Create initial commit on master (go-git default)
  1954→	testFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "test.txt")
  1955→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("initial"), 0o644); err != nil {
  1956→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
  1957→	}
  1958→	if _, err := w.Add("test.txt"); err != nil {
  1959→		t.Fatalf("failed to add test file: %v", err)
  1960→	}
  1961→	mainCommit, err := w.Commit("main commit with Entire-Checkpoint: abc123def456", &git.CommitOptions{
  1962→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@example.com"},
  1963→	})
  1964→	if err != nil {
  1965→		t.Fatalf("failed to create main commit: %v", err)
  1966→	}
  1967→
  1968→	// Create feature branch
  1969→	featureBranch := "feature/test"
  1970→	if err := w.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{
  1971→		Hash:   mainCommit,
  1972→		Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(featureBranch),
  1973→		Create: true,
  1974→	}); err != nil {
  1975→		t.Fatalf("failed to create feature branch: %v", err)
  1976→	}
  1977→
  1978→	// Create commit on feature branch
  1979→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("feature work"), 0o644); err != nil {
  1980→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
  1981→	}
  1982→	if _, err := w.Add("test.txt"); err != nil {
  1983→		t.Fatalf("failed to add test file: %v", err)
  1984→	}
  1985→	_, err = w.Commit("feature commit with Entire-Checkpoint: def456ghi789", &git.CommitOptions{
  1986→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@example.com"},
  1987→	})
  1988→	if err != nil {
  1989→		t.Fatalf("failed to create feature commit: %v", err)
  1990→	}
  1991→
  1992→	// Create .entire directory
  1993→	if err := os.MkdirAll(".entire", 0o750); err != nil {
  1994→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .entire dir: %v", err)
  1995→	}
  1996→
  1997→	// Get checkpoints - should only include feature branch commits, not main
  1998→	// Note: Without actual checkpoint data in entire/checkpoints/v1, this returns empty
  1999→	// but the important thing is it doesn't error and the filtering logic runs
  2000→	points, err := getBranchCheckpoints(repo, 20)
  2001→	if err != nil {
  2002→		t.Fatalf("getBranchCheckpoints() error = %v", err)
  2003→	}
  2004→
  2005→	// Without checkpoint data (no entire/checkpoints/v1 branch), should return 0 checkpoints
  2006→	// This validates the filtering code path runs without error
  2007→	if len(points) != 0 {
  2008→		t.Errorf("expected 0 checkpoints without checkpoint data, got %d", len(points))
  2009→	}
  2010→}

  2014→	fullTranscript := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"prompt 1"}}
  2015→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"response 1"}]}}
  2016→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"prompt 2"}}
  2017→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"response 2"}]}}
  2018→{"type":"user","uuid":"u3","message":{"content":"prompt 3"}}

  2020→

Good. Now let me implement the `walkFirstParentCommits` helper and modify all three functions. I'll do this step by step.

First, add the `walkFirstParentCommits` helper function:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01BHwbbjDYx5NTHP8cY1jdQK
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/cmd/entire/cli/explain.go",
  "old_string": "// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early\nvar errStopIteration = errors.New(\"stop iteration\")",
  "new_string": "// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early\nvar errStopIteration = errors.New(\"stop iteration\")\n\n// walkFirstParentCommits walks the first-parent chain starting from `from`,\n// calling fn for each commit. It stops after visiting `limit` commits (0 = no limit).\n// This avoids the full DAG traversal that repo.Log() does, which follows ALL parents\n// of merge commits and can walk into unrelated branch history (e.g., main's full\n// history after merging main into a feature branch).\nfunc walkFirstParentCommits(repo *git.Repository, from plumbing.Hash, limit int, fn func(*object.Commit) error) error {\n\tcurrent, err := repo.CommitObject(from)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit %s: %w\", from, err)\n\t}\n\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor {\n\t\tif limit > 0 && count >= limit {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount++\n\n\t\tif err := fn(current); err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Follow first parent only (skip merge parents)\n\t\tif current.NumParents() == 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tparent, parentErr := current.Parent(0)\n\t\tif parentErr != nil {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tcurrent = parent\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}
```

branch first: refs/heads/<name>\n\t\t\tref, refErr := repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName(\"refs/heads/\"+defaultBranchName), true)\n\t\t\tif refErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Fall back to remote default branch: refs/remotes/origin/<name>\n\t\t\t\tref, refErr = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName(\"refs/remotes/origin/\"+defaultBranchName), true)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif refErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tmainBranchHash = ref.Hash()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Fall back to main/master detection if default branch resolution failed\n\t\tif mainBranchHash == plumbing.ZeroHash {\n\t\t\tmainBranchHash = strategy.GetMainBranchHash(repo)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Precompute commits reachable from main (for O(1) filtering instead of O(N) per commit)\n\t// This changes the filtering from O(N*M) to O(N+M) where N=commits scanned, M=main history depth\n\treachableFromMain := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)\n\tif mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {\n\t\tmainIter, mainErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: mainBranchHash})\n\t\tif mainErr == nil {\n\t\t\tmainCount := 0\n\t\t\t_ = mainIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort\n\t\t\t\tmainCount++\n\t\t\t\tif mainCount > 1000 { // Same depth limit as before\n\t\t\t\t\treturn errStopIteration\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treachableFromMain[c.Hash] = true\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tmainIter.Close()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Walk git history and collect checkpoints\n\titer, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{\n\t\tFrom:  head.Hash(),\n\t\tOrder: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit log: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer iter.Close()\n\n\tvar points []strategy.RewindPoint\n\tcount := 0\n\tconsecutiveMainCount := 0\n\n\terr = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {\n\t\tif count >= commitScanLimit {\n\t\t\treturn errStopIteration\n\t\t}\n\t\tcount++\n\n\t\t// On feature branches, skip commits that are reachable from main\n\t\t// (but continue scanning - there may be more feature branch commits)\n\t\tif len(reachableFromMain) > 0 {\n\t\t\tif reachableFromMain[c.Hash] {\n\t\t\t\tconsecutiveMainCount++\n\t\t\t\tif consecutiveMainCount >= consecutiveMainLimit {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn errStopIteration // Likely exhausted feature branch commits\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn nil // Skip this commit, continue scanning\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tconsecutiveMainCount = 0 // Reset on feature branch commit\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer\n\t\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)\n\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\treturn nil // No checkpoint trailer, continue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Look up checkpoint info\n\t\tcpInfo, found := committedByID[cpID]\n\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\treturn nil // Checkpoint not in store, continue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create rewind point from committed info\n\t\tmessage := strings.Split(c.Message, \"\\n\")[0]\n\t\tpoint := strategy.RewindPoint{\n\t\t\tID:               c.Hash.String(),\n\t\t\tMessage:          message,\n\t\t\tDate:             c.Committer.When,\n\t\t\tIsLogsOnly:       true, // Committed checkpoints are logs-only\n\t\t\tCheckpointID:     cpID,\n\t\t\tSessionID:        cpInfo.SessionID,\n\t\t\tIsTaskCheckpoint: cpInfo.IsTask,\n\t\t\tToolUseID:        cpInfo.ToolUseID,\n\t\t\tAgent:            cpInfo.Agent,\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Read session prompt from metadata branch (best-effort)\n\t\tcontent, _ := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID) //nolint:errcheck  // Best-effort\n\t\tif content != nil {\n\t\t\t// Scope the transcript to this checkpoint's portion\n\t\t\t// If CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0, we slice the transcript to only include\n\t\t\t// lines from that point onwards (excluding earlier checkpoint content)\n\t\t\tscopedTranscript := scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(content.Transcript, content.Metadata.GetTranscriptStart())\n\t\t\t// Extract prompts from the scoped transcript (not the full session's prompts)\n\t\t\tscopedPrompts := extractPromptsFromTranscript(scopedTranscript)\n\t\t\tif len(scopedPrompts) > 0 && scopedPrompts[0] != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tpoint.SessionPrompt = scopedPrompts[0]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpoints = append(points, point)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\n\tif err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error iterating commits: %w\", err)\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\t// Check if we're on the default branch (needed for getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints)\n\tisOnDefault, _ := strategy.IsOnDefaultBranch(repo)\n\n\t// Walk first-parent chain to collect checkpoints.\n\t// First-parent traversal follows only the \"main line\" of commits on this branch,\n\t// skipping merge parents. This avoids walking into main's full history when main\n\t// has been merged into a feature branch.\n\tvar points []strategy.RewindPoint\n\n\terr = walkFirstParentCommits(repo, head.Hash(), commitScanLimit, func(c *object.Commit) error {\n\t\t// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer\n\t\tcpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)\n\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\treturn nil // No checkpoint trailer, continue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Look up checkpoint info\n\t\tcpInfo, found := committedByID[cpID]\n\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\treturn nil // Checkpoint not in store, continue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create rewind point from committed info\n\t\tmessage := strings.Split(c.Message, \"\\n\")[0]\n\t\tpoint := strategy.RewindPoint{\n\t\t\tID:               c.Hash.String(),\n\t\t\tMessage:          message,\n\t\t\tDate:             c.Committer.When,\n\t\t\tIsLogsOnly:       true, // Committed checkpoints are logs-only\n\t\t\tCheckpointID:     cpID,\n\t\t\tSessionID:        cpInfo.SessionID,\n\t\t\tIsTaskCheckpoint: cpInfo.IsTask,\n\t\t\tToolUseID:        cpInfo.ToolUseID,\n\t\t\tAgent:            cpInfo.Agent,\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Read session prompt from metadata branch (best-effort)\n\t\tcontent, _ := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID) //nolint:errcheck  // Best-effort\n\t\tif content != nil {\n\t\t\t// Scope the transcript to this checkpoint's portion\n\t\t\tscopedTranscript := scopeTranscriptForCheckpoint(content.Transcript, content.Metadata.GetTranscriptStart())\n\t\t\tscopedPrompts := extractPromptsFromTranscript(scopedTranscript)\n\t\t\tif len(scopedPrompts) > 0 && scopedPrompts[0] != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tpoint.SessionPrompt = scopedPrompts[0]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpoints = append(points, point)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"error iterating commits: %w\", err)\n\t}"

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to checkout feature branch: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create merge commit: merge main into feature (feature is first parent, main is second parent)\n\tfeatureCommitObj, _ := repo.CommitObject(featureCommit1)\n\tfeatureTree, _ := featureCommitObj.Tree()\n\tmergeHash := createMergeCommit(t, repo, featureCommit1, mainCommit, featureTree.Hash, \"Merge branch 'master' into feature/test\")\n\n\t// Update feature branch ref to point to merge commit\n\tref := plumbing.NewHashReference(featureBranch, mergeHash)\n\tif err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to reset to merge: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create second feature checkpoint commit (AFTER the merge)\n\tcpID2 := id.MustCheckpointID(\"ccc333ddd444\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"feature work 2\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create feature branch: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create feature checkpoint commit\n\tcpID := id.MustCheckpointID(\"eee555fff666\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"feature work\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := w.Add(\"test.txt\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to add test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tfeatureCommit, err := w.Commit(trailers.FormatCheckpoint(\"feat: feature work\", cpID), &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@example.com\", When: time.Now().Add(-3 * time.Hour)},\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create feature commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Switch to master and add a commit\n\tif err := w.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{\n\t\tBranch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(\"master\"),\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to checkout master: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tmainFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \"main.txt\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(mainFile, []byte(\"main work\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write main file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := w.Add(\"main.txt\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to add main file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tmainCommit, err := w.Commit(\"main: add work\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@example.com\", When: time.Now().Add(-2 * time.Hour)},\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create main commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Switch back to feature and create merge commit AT HEAD\n\tif err := w.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{\n\t\tBranch: featureBranch,\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to update feature branch ref: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create .entire directory\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(\".entire\", 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .entire dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// HEAD is the merge commit itself.\n\t// getAssociatedCommits should walk: merge -> featureCommit -> initial\n\t// and find the checkpoint on featureCommit.\n\tcommits, err := getAssociatedCommits(repo, cpID, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"getAssociatedCommits error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(commits) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 associated commit when HEAD is merge commit, got %d\", len(commits))\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(commits[0].Message, \"feat: feature work\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected feature commit message, got %q\", commits[0].Message)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestWalkFirstParentCommits_SkipsMergeParents(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Verify that walkFirstParentCommits follows only first parents and doesn't\n\t// enter the second parent (merge source) of merge commits.\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(tmpDir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainInit(tmpDir, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init git repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tw, err := repo.Worktree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get worktree: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create initial commit (shared ancestor)\n\ttestFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \"test.txt\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"initial\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create feature branch: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"feature\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := w.Add(\"test.txt\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to add test file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tfeatureCommit, err := w.Commit(\"B: feature work\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@example.com\", When: time.Now().Add(-4 * time.Hour)},\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create feature commit: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create main branch commit (will be merge source)\n\tif err := w.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{\n\t\tBranch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(\"master\"),\n\t});
